Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the leptons object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 16. The moduli space has dimension at most 529 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by leptons generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 16, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 529. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any leptons module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 649. The projective resolution has length at most 515 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.
